MEETING: Sine Die | DATE: 2023-01-03 | CLIP_ID: 878 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 878

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
 OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
 Office of City Clerk 
 
 January 3, 2023  
           
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in “Sine Die” Session on this date at 12:00 
p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Those present were: MAYOR: 
Patrick Collins; COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, Richard 
Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White.  Also present: 
Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited.   
 
 Following the pledge of allegiance, Mayor Collins stated the purpos e of the session was to 
dissolve the current governing body as well as recognize the outgoing City Council President for 
2023.     
 
 Mayor Collins expressed appreciation to members of the City Council, appointed officials, 
city staff, residents of the City  of Cheyenne and Honorable Justice Kate Vox, Wyoming Supreme 
Court.     
 
Mayor Collins expressed appreciation to Mr. Roybal for his leadership as City Council 
President for 2022.  Mr. Roybal commented on his service as City Council President for the past 
year. 
 
Mayor Collins announced the First Regular Meeting of the governing body would convene 
in City Council Chambers at 12:15 p.m.   
 
 There being no further business previously noted for the “Sine Die” Session, Mayor Collins 
adjourned the meeting at 12:04 p.m. 
 
       Submitted by, 
 
 
       Kristina F. Jones 
       City Clerk 
 
 This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
 
PUBLISHED: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  January 7, 2023

MEETING: First Regular Meeting | DATE: 2023-01-03 | CLIP_ID: 879 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 879

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
            
January 3, 2023  
 
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne assembled for its First Regular Meeting for 
2023 on this date in Council Chambers  and via electronic conference meeting .  Welcoming 
comments were made by Mayor Collins. 
 
 Mayor Collins officially called the First Regular Meeting of the governing body to order 
at 12:15 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Those present were: 
MAYOR – Patrick Collins; COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel, Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White.  Also present was Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited.   
 
 Election of City Council President for 2023.  Mayor Collins stated that, pursuant to City 
Code, the mayor conducts the election for Council President but does not cast a vote.  Dr. Rinne 
moved to nominate and elect  Mr. Johnson as City Council President for 2023, seconded by Mr.  
Roybal.   Motion to nominate and elect Mr. Johnson as City Council President carried.    Voting 
“yes” -- all members of the governing body with the exception of  Mr. Segrave voting “no” and 
Mayor Collins who did not vote. 
 
 Election of City Council Vice President for 2023.  Mayor Collins stated that, pursuant to 
City Code, the mayor conducts the election for Council Vice -President but does not cast a vote.  
Dr. Rinne moved to nominate and elect M r. Esquibel as City Council Vice President for 2023, 
seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion to nominate and elect M r. Esquibel as City Council Vice 
President carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Mayor 
Collins who did not vote.  
 
 Approval of appointment of City Officials (pursuant to Title 2 of the Code of the City of 
Cheyenne) as follows: Charles W. Bloom, Planning and Development Director; Stefanie Boster, 
City Attorney; Bradley M. Bowen, Assistant City Treasurer (Board of Public Utilities) ; Thomas 
D.  Cobb, City Engineer; Mark Francisco, Chief of the Cheyenne Police Department ; Ronnald J. 
Jeffrey, Juvenile Court Judge; Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk/Assistant City Treasurer; John B. 
Kopper, Chief of Cheyenne Fire and Rescue Department; Robin L. Lockman, City Treasurer;  
Vicki Nemecek, Director of Public Works; Anthony R oss, Senior Municipal Court Judge; Jason 
M. Sanchez, Community Recreation & Events Director; and David E. Singleton, Municipal Court 
Judge.  Mayor Collins stated that, pursuant to state law, as these are Mayoral appointees, he would 
not be participating in discussion or voting on the proposed appointments.  Mr. Roybal moved to 
approve the appointees “en masse”, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried with all members of 
the governing body voting “yes” with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote.  Following 
announcement of the vote, Honorable Justice Kate Fox, Wyoming Supreme Court, administered 
the oath of appointed office to the City Officials. 
 
   
 
 There being no further business previously noted for the First Regular Meeting and purpose 
of the meeting have been met, the meeting was officially adjourned at 12:23 p.m. 
 
       Submitted by, 
 
 
       Kristina F. Jones, 
       City Clerk 
 
 This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
   
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  January 7, 2023

The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning

Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn. Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White . 
Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no

by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Upon request by Dr. Aldrich, and pursuant to support 
received by Mr. Johnson and Mr. White, items #24 , #28(d) and #28(e) were removed from the 
Consent Agenda.  Upon request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich 
and Mr. Johnson, items #28(b) and #28(c) were removed from the Consent Agenda.   Mr. Roybal 
moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present.

conditions required by Wyoming Statute § 15 -1-402 exist for about 10.7 acres of land located at

No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made  that due to the Martin Luther King Jr. holiday, regularly 
scheduled City Council Committee meetings will instead be held follows:  Finance Committee 
will meet Tuesday, January 17, 2023 at Noon and Public Services Committee will meet 
Wednesday, January 18, 2023 at Noon.  Both meetings will be held at 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Room 
104 and via electronic conference meeting.   
 
Announcement was made that two Calcuttas benefitting Make-A-Wish Wyoming, will be 
held January 27 – January 29, 2023 at VFW 1881, 2826 E. 7 th St., Cheyenne, WY and March 14 
– March 19, 2023 at the Red Lion Hotel, 204 Fox Farm Rd., Cheyenne, WY (15% of proceeds go 
to the charity). 
 
Public comment was made by Jason Sanchez under Other Business.

(license currently held by Poor Richard’s d/b/a Poor Richard’s).

Aldrich. 
 
Mr. Johnson mo ved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16 -4-
405(a)(vii)(x), seconded by Mr. Esquibel. Motion carried .  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open 
to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following  were in attendance:  Gene MacDonald, GLM Design 
Group, LLC, Mathew Whipple, DHM Design Corporation, City Attorney, City Clerk, City 
Treasurer, Fire Chief, Chief of Staff and members of Planning and Development. (Clerk’s Note: 
executive session convened at 8:30 p.m.)

officially adjourned at 9:31 p.m. 
         
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones,

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
February 6, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in a special session on this date 
beginning at 12:11 p.m. in Committee Room 104 and via electronic conference meeting.  Present 
were: COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook,  Ken Esquibel, Richard 
Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent: MAYOR – Patrick 
Collins and Dr. Mark Rinne. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.   
 
 RESOLUTION -- Authorizing the submission of an application to the State Loan and 
Investment Board for a grant th rough the Local Government Project ARP A grant program on 
behalf of the Governing Body for the City of Cheyenne fo r the purpose of providing critically 
needed flood control at East Dell Range Boulevard and Van Buren Avenue. Reporting for the 
Finance Committee, Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Royal.  Motion carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Comment was made by the following member 
of the governing body: Mr. Laybourn.  (#6299) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Supporting the Board of Public Utilities’ application to the Wyoming 
State Loan and Investment Board for a Local Government ARPA Grant for the Water Reclamation 
Facilities (WRF) Hydraulic Capacity Project (Project) in the amount of $1,984,000.00 a nd 
authorizing the Director of the Board of Public Utilities to execute the application and all related 
documents.  Mr. White advised this item was reviewed under other items for the agenda at the 
February 6, 2023 Finance Committee meeting, therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Code have 
been met.  Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion  carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body present.  Comment was made by the following member of 
the governing body: Mr. Laybourn.  (#6300)  
  
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 12:14 p.m. 
         
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones,    
City Clerk

The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning

– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook,  Ken Esquibel, 
Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White .  Absent: Richard 
Johnson. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Mr. Segrave moved to approve, seconded by  Mr. White.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on January 23, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried.

Consent Agenda) carried.

application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Wyoming Horse Racing, LLC d/b/a Horse Palace, 
6421 Speer Rd., Cheyenne, WY (license currently held by Poor Richard’s d/b/a Poor Richard’s).

application to Finance Committee.  Public comment was made by: Nick Hughes, President of 
Wyoming Horse Racing, LLC.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, and Mr. Laybourn.

Segrave.  Mr. Cook moved to postpone to February 27, 2023, seconded by Mr.  Segrave. Motion

§15-1-402 exist for 9.87 acres of land situated in portions of Sunnyside Addi tion and Sunnyside

MEETING: Special City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-02-16 | CLIP_ID: 893 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 893

   
RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
February 16, 2023  
 
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in special session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Committee Room 104, 2101 O’Neil Ave, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for the purpose of collective labor bargaining with the Cheyenne Professional Fire fighters, 
International Association of Firefighters, Local No. 279.  Present were: MAYOR – Patrick Collins, 
COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook (arriving at 6:34 p.m. during 
executive session), Ken Esquibel, Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, 
Tom Segrave and Jeff White.  Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk; Stefanie Boster, City 
Attorney; Jo Jordan, Legal Assistant, City Attorney’s Office; Robin Lockman, City Treasurer, and 
Chief John Kopper, Cheyenne Fire and Rescue.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Mayor Collins advised the meeting would be audio recorded and available on Facebook 
live and stated that Wyoming State Statute §16-4-405(a)(x) of the Public Meetings Act allows the 
governing body to meet in executive session to consider or tender offers regarding salaries, wages 
or other terms and conditions of employment during negotiations with the fire fighters union and 
that any motion approved by the governing body to move into executive session during the special 
meetings would be pursuant to this section of statute.    
 
Mayor Collins provided  opening comments noting he has been meeting with the Fire 
Department for labor management discussions which have resulted in a better understanding of 
the functions of their department.  Mayor Collins expressed appreciation to Chief John Kopper for 
the work he does with the Union and his representation of the Fire Department and to members of 
the Union’s negotiation team.  The proposed Ground Rules were agreed upon and signed by the 
City and Union. [Cle rk’s note: City’s Spokespersons are: Mayor Patrick Collins, City Attorney 
Stefanie Boster and City Council President Richard Johnson.  Union’s Spokespersons are: Neil 
Durante, Vincent McQueeney and Kelsey Raile.]  
 
Following signing of the Ground Rules by Mayor Collins and Union Spokesperson Vincent 
McQueeney both parties presented their respective proposed Collective Bargaining Agreement 
proposals.  Mayor Collins briefly reviewed the City’s proposal which included changes to the 
following areas and Sections: update cover page; update table of contents; Section 2 -Definitions; 
Section 3-Exclusive Bargaining Agent ; Section 4 -Term of Agreement ; Section 8 -Grievance 
Procedures; Section 12 -Union Representation ; Section 13 -Management Rights ; Section 15 -
Department Staffing; Section 19 -Inclement Weather; Section 21-Classification of Employment 
and Basic Wages; Section 22-Overtime; Section 23-Working in a Higher Job Rank ; Section 24-
Conversion of Benefits ; Section 2 6-Vacation/EOD; Section 31 -Uniforms; Section 32 -Personal 
Protective Equipment; and Section 42-Longevity.  Mr. McQueeney briefly reviewed the Union’s 
proposal which included changes to the following areas and Sections: update cover page; update 
table of con tents; Section 2 -Definitions, Section 15-Department Staffing; Section 20 -Holidays; 
   
Section 21 -Classification of Employment and Basic Wages; Section 22 -Overtime; Section 23 -
Working in a Higher Job Rank; and Section 26-Vacation/EOD  
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  present.  Those present in executive session 
were:  All members of the governing body; Stefanie Boster, City Attorney; Kristina F. Jones, City 
Clerk; Robin Lockman, City Treasurer; Chief John Kopper, Cheyenne Fire & Rescue; and Jo 
Jordan, City Attorney Legal Assistant.  [Clerk’s Note: executive session convened at 6:27 p.m.] 
 
 Following closure of the executive session, Mayor Collins reconvened the regular meeting 
at 7:02 p.m.  Union Spokesperson Mr. McQueeney echoed the importance of the labor 
management discussions  that occur throughout the year leading up to negotiations.  Mr. 
McQueeney announced the Union’s acceptance of the City’s proposed agreement as written.  
Mayor Collins announced the City’s acceptance of the Union’s proposed agreement as written.  
 
  There bein g no fur ther business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:05 p.m.  
 
 
       Submitted by, 
        
        
        
       Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk  
 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
 
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  February 25, 2023

[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the City Treasurer to discharge uncollectible debts pursuant

Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Main motion

Memorandum of Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Downtown      
Development Authority (DDA) to provide professional and administrative services.  Reporting for

been met.  Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by Mr . Roybal.  Mr. White moved to amend 
by substitute dated March 9, 2023, seconded by Mr . Roybal .  Motion to amend by substitute  
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Main motion as amended by 
substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. Public comment 
was made by: Wendy Volk.  Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: 
Mr. Laybourn. [Clerk’s note: substitute is dated March 8, 2023] (#7704)

Superday, Superday Tour de Prairie and the Superday 5K walk/run (Revenue to the City) – referred 
to Finance Committee.

for constructing, reconstructing, maintaining, operating, repairing, inspecting, surveying, altering,

Roofing, Inc., for the Sanitation Transfer Station Reroof Project (Solid Waste Fund).  Motion (per

[CA] Consideration to purchase two (2) used SUV’s for the City of Cheyenne, Compliance

Landfill.  (Solid Waste Fund) – referred to Finance Committee.    
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E-15-23 for 95 gallon and 65 gallon automated refuse carts for the 
City of Cheyenne Sanitation Division (Solid Waste Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
         
[CA] Consideration to Purchase between the City of Cheyenne and Front Range Fire Apparatus 
for the purchase of one (1) BME Fire Trucks, LLC, BME Freightliner Model 34 Targhee Type 3,

Renewal applications for existing City liquor licens es (retail, restaurant, limited retail, 
resort, bar and grill licenses, microbrewery, satellite distillery, special malt beverage and winery 
permits) filed with the City Clerk’s Office.  Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  
Mr. White moved to divide the question to consider renewal applications for Poor Richard’s and 
the Dog Haus separately, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion to divide the question carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Mr.  White moved to approve the renewal 
retail liquor license for Poor Richard’s and the renewal bar & grill liquor license for Dog Haus. 
Motion died due to lack of a second.  Main motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: 
Mayor Collins and Mr. White. 
  
Appointments of Danica Mrozinsky, Amber Nuse and Jamie Winters to the Downtown 
Development Authority Board.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion 
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present with the exception of Mayor 
Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
            
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Under other business: 
 
Mr. Johnson, having voted on the prevailing side and pursuant to City Code Chapter 2.04, 
moved to reconsider the vote taken at the February 27, 2023 City Council meeting for the Donation 
Agreement with Union Pacific Railroad Company to accept a donation of selected fleet rail road 
cars, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion to reconsider carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present. Mr. Johnson moved to amend by substitute dated March 9, 2023, 
seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion to amend by substitute carried. Voting “yes” – all members of 
the governing body present with the exception of Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Laybourn voting “no”.  Main 
motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present with the exception of Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook and Mr. Laybourn voting “no”.  Comments 
were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel and Mr. 
Laybourn.  (#7694) 
 
 Mr. White, having voted on the prevailing side and pursuant to City Code Chapter 2.04, 
moved to reconsider the vote taken at the February 27, 2023 City Council meeting for the 
Resolution authorizing PaydHealth, LLC, to provide advocacy and financial case management 
services for eligible specialty prescription drugs through alternative funding programs effective 
July 1, 2023, seconded by Mr. Segrave. Mr. Johnson declared a conflict of interest and vacated 
council chambers.  Motion to reconsider carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body present in council chambers.  Mr. White moved to postpone to March 27, 2023, seconded by 
Dr. Aldrich.  Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” – all members present in council chambers. 
 
 Public comment was by the following individuals under other business: Jim Ridgeway, 
Emily Intlekofer, Black Dog Animal Rescue, Nick L umbreraz, Patrick Rudd, Amanda Ellis, 
Diamond in the Rough Pet Recovery, Shawn Giffin- Dean, Wendy Volk, Tony Brannon, Chelsea 
Fletcher, Maddi Ha ak, Black Dog Animal Rescue, Amy Buxton, Wayne Lilly, Joan Franklin, 
Serena Smith, Joe McCord, Linda Burt, Bethany Jacobs, Jason Dean, Gail Young, Alyssa Brown, 
Michael White, Dr. Tessa Win sch, Cheyenne Animal Shelter, Rick Redella,, Britney Tennant, 
CEO, Cheyenne Animal Shelter, Suzanne Jones and Richard Lysle.    
 
 The following members of the governing body made comments under other business: Dr. 
Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Esquibel, and Mr. Johnson.  
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 9:58 p.m. 
         
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kylie Soden,    
Deputy City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request.

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-03-27 | CLIP_ID: 899 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 899

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
March 27, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave  and Jeff White. Absent: Pete 
Laybourn. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Dr. Rinne, and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Johnson and Mr. Cook, item #19(d) was removed from the Consent Agenda. Mr. White 
moved to approve the remaining items, seconded by Dr. Aldrich. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body present. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on March 13 , 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried.  
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, tracts of 
land situated in portions of Sunny Side Addition and Sunnyside Addition Second Filing, Section 
12, T. 13 N., R. 67 W., 6 th P.M., Laramie County Wyoming, generally located east of Ridge Rd. 
between Charles St. and Pershing Blvd., west of Wills Rd. between Cheyenne St. and Pershing 
Blvd., and northeast of the intersection of Pershing Blvd. and Wills Rd.  Mr. Cook moved to 
approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present. (#4495) 
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the Zoning Classification from County MU Mixed Use to MUB Mixed- Use 
Business Emphasis and CB Community Business for tracts of land situated in portions of Sunny 
Side Addition and Sunnyside Addition Second Filing, Section 12, T. 13 N., R. 67 W., 6 th P.M., 
Laramie County, Wyoming, generally located east of Ridge Rd. between Charles St. and Pershing 
Blvd., west of Wills Rd. between Cheyenne St. and Pershing Blvd., and northeast of the  
intersection of Pershing Blvd. and Wills Rd.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final 
reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body present. (#4496)  
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, land 
located west of Southwest Drive south of I -80. Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, 
seconded by Mr. Segrave . Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present. Public comment was made by : Ben Hornok, Shane Hansen , Steli Surveying Services, 
agent for the applicant , and Mike White . Comment was made by the following member s of the 
governing body: Mayor Collins and Dr. Rinne. 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from County LR Low -Density Residential to LR 
Low-Density Residential for land located west of Southwest Drive south of I-80. Mr. Cook moved 
to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present.  
 
 ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Repealing and recreating code provisions prohibiting 
drug paraphernalia as specified, and amending Section 9.20.020, Narcotics and Drugs, of Chapter 
9.20, Controlled Substances, of Title 9, Public Peace and Welfare, of the Municipal Code of the 
City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, to eliminate municipal prohibitions governing marijuana . Mr.  
Johnson moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Roybal .  Motion failed .  Voting 
“yes” – Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, and Mr. Roybal.  Voting “no” – Mayor Collins, Dr. Aldrich, 
Mr. Cook. Dr. Rinne, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White. Public  comment was made by: Ben Hornok, 
Max Esdale, Rhea Parsons, Matt Freeman, A.J. Gold, Kathleen Peterson, and Arnold Strayer.  
Comment was made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, 
and Mr. Johnson.  
 
 RESOLUTION – Authorizing the City Treasurer to discharge uncollectible debts pursuant 
to W.S. § 16-4-502. Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Aldrich and Dr. Rinne voting 
“no”. Comment was made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich and Dr. 
Rinne. (#6312)  
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Expressing the will of the Governing Body to prioritize and adequately 
fund specified downtown 6 th Penny Tax Maintenance and Improvement Projects . Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. (#6313) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor to enter into a Memorandum of Understanding 
with Laramie County, Albany County, and the City of Laramie allocating Impact Assistance Funds 
resulting from an application to construct and operate a mine filed by the Gold King Corp. before 
the Industrial Siting Division. Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried. (#6314) 
 
RESOLUTION – Authorizing Paydhealth, LLC, to provide advocacy and financial case 
management services for eligible specialty prescription drugs through alternative funding 
programs effective July 1, 2023. (Postponed from March 13, 2023) . Mr. Johnson declared a 
conflict of interest and vacated council chambers.  Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. 
Segrave. Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present with the 
exception of Mr. Cook and Mr. Esquibel voting “no”.   Comment was made by the following 
members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. White. (#6310)  
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Providing for the adoption of a Fund Balance Policy in accordance with

[CA]  RESOLUTION – Adopting the Wyoming Region 7 Hazard Mitigation Plan 2023 Update. 
-Referred to Finance Committee. (Sponsor- Mr. White) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor to execute settlement participation forms for the 
City of Cheyenne to participate in the Settlement Agreements designated as the “Teva, Allergan, 
CVS, Walgreens, Walmart National Opioid Settlement”. (Revenue to City) – Referred to Finance

[CA] Sponsorship Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and HollyFrontier  Cheyenne 
Renewable Diesel Company, LLC to designate HollyFrontier as the presenting sponsor of 2023 
Superday, Superday Tour de Prairie and the Superday 5K walk/run (Revenue to the City) Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. (#7714) 
         
[CA] Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Umpires Association for the 
2023 and 2024 Softball seasons  (Recreation Programs Fund) Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried. (#7715) 
 
[CA] Right-of-Way License Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Bent 15, LLC for a 
portion of Lots 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17 and 18, Block 413, Original City of Cheyenne, Laramie 
County, Wyoming, known at 1509 Bent Avenue. Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried. (#7716) 
 
 Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and TDSi -  The Design 
Studio, inc., for the Gymnastics Facility Site Development, Engineering and Architectural Plans 
Project (Recreation Programs Fund) . Mr. White moved to approve  in an amount not to exceed 
$293,844.00, seconded by Mr. Esquibel. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body with the exception of Dr. Rinne voting “no”. Public comment was made by: Mike 
White and Randy Byers, TDSi – The Design Studio, inc . Comment was made by the following 
members of the governing body: Mayor Collins, Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook, and Dr. Rinne. (#7717)  
   
[CA] Easement Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Visionary Communications LLC 
for constructing, reconstructing, maintaining, operating, repairing, inspecting, surveying, altering, 
abandoning, replacing and/or removing fiber optic cable and related material at 514 West Fox  
Farm Road (Revenue to the City) Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried. (#7718) 
          
 Lease Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Meals on Wheels of Cheyenne, Inc. 
for a portion of the property located at 2015 S. Greeley Hwy (General Fund). Mayor Collins asked 
to vote down the lease agreement. Mr. White moved to approve . Motion died due to lack of a 
second. Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: Mayor Collins.   
 
[CA] Business Associate Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne Regional 
Medical Center, and Dr. Clair White for the Behavioral Health Co -Response Project.  (Wyoming 
Department of Health Grant) -Referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Professional Services Contract between the City of Cheyenne and Kyle M. Brandon for 
Public Defender Services.  (General Fund) -Referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and The Church of St. Mary 
for Olivet Cemetery grounds maintenance, irrigation, operations and administrative services.  
(Revenue to the City) -Referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Development Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Whitney Ranch 3 rd and 4th 
Filing, LLC, to outline responsibilities for public infrastructure improvements relating to Whitney 
Ranch 4th Filing Plat. -Referred to Public Services Committee.                  
                  
[CA] Contract Modification #1 to Contract #7321 for the Development Agreement between the 
City of Cheyenne and Whitney Ranch, LLC, to outline responsibilities for public infrastructure 
improvements related to Whitney Ranch 3 rd and 6 th Filings. -Referred to Public Services 
Committee. 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E-13-23 for ten (10) portable litter fences for the City of Cheyenne 
Landfill.  (Solid Waste Fund)  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried ( in an amount not to exceed 
$79,000.00).  
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E-15-23 for 95 gallon and 65 gallon automated refus e carts for the 
City of Cheyenne Sanitation Division (Solid Waste Fund) Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried 
(in an amount not to exceed $282,845.00).  
 
[CA] Consideration to Purchase between the City of Cheyenne and Front Range Fire Apparatus 
for the purchase of one (1) BME Fire Trucks, LLC, BME Freightliner Model 34 Targhee Type 3, 
4x4 Fire Engine (2021 Specific Purpose Option Tax Fund) Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried 
(in an amount not to exceed $428,000.00).  
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #S-17-23 for an Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and S & 
S Builders, LLC for the Pioneer Park Hip Roof Shelter.  (Development Impact Fees & Community

[CA] Consideration of Bid #E-3-23 for an Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Goat

County Weed & Pest Grant) -Referred to Finance Committee. 
 
Appointments of Elizabeth Haigler to the Housing and Community Development Advisory 
Council and Mackenzie Howard to the Community Action of Laramie County Board of Directors. 
Dr. Al drich moved to approve, seconded by Mr. White. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present with the exception of Mayor Collins (mayoral appointments).  
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented.

for a winery permit application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for The Blue Stem Wine House,

402 exist for about 1.29 acres of land at 5224 Ridge Road (located wes t of Ridge Road, between

April 10, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave  and Jeff White. 
Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Upon request by Mr. Segrave, and pursuant to support 
received by Mr.  Roybal and Mr. Laybourn, item # 14 was removed from the Consent Agenda.  
Upon request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Segrave,

carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Rinne voting 
“no”. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on March 27, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried.

Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a winery permit application filed with the City

hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing and referred the application to

Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-04-24 | CLIP_ID: 903 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 903

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
April 24, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook,  Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne,  Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent: Scott 
Roybal. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Upon request by Mr.  White, and pursuant to support 
received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Cook, item #18(a) was removed from the Consent Agenda. Mr. 
Segrave moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body present with the exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no”. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regu lar Meeting of the Governing Body on April 10, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for disposal of real property and land exchange 
between the City of Cheyenne and Sun Valley Estates LLC (generally located south of Raleigh 
Drive).  There being no comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor 
Collins closed the public hearing.   
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a bar and grill application filed with the City 
Clerk’s Office for FAFO Restaurant Group LLC d/b/a Dog Haus Restaurant, 3838 Atkins St., 
Cheyenne, WY.  Public comment was made by:  Marlena and Charles  Schultz. Comments were 
made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook and Mr. Esquibel. 
There being no further comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor 
Collins closed the public hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for the City of Cheyenne’s Annual Action Plan 
Program Year 2023 prepared by the Cheyenne Housing and Community Development office.  
Comment was made by the following member of the governing body:  Dr. Aldrich.  There being 
no further comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed 
the public hearing. 
 
 
 
 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, land 
located west of Ridge Road, between Jackson Street and Douglas Street . Reporting for Public 
Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr.  Segrave. 
Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Public comment was 
made by: Michael White.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: 
Mayor Collins and Mr. Cook. 
  
  ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne establishing the zoning classification of MR Medium-Density Residential for land being 
annexed to the City of Cheyenne located at 5224 Ridge Road (west of Ridge Road, between 
Jackson Street and Douglas Street).  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by 
Mr. Segrave. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  present.  
Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich.   
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from MR Medium -Density Residential to NR -2 
Neighborhood Residential - Medium Density for land located on Lots 1 and 2, Block 1, Twin 22 
Subdivision.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr.  Segrave. Motion 
carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  present.  Public comment was made 
by: Brandon Swain, landowner.  Comment was made by the following member of the governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Chapter 2.04, Mayor and City Council, 
within Title 2, Administration and Personnel, and Chapter 12.04, Streets, Sidewalks and 
Driveways Generally, within Title 12, Streets, Sidewalks and Public Places, of the Municipal Code

ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Ordinance No. 4429 updating the City of

Comments were made by the following members of the governing body:  Mayor Collins, Mr.

The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave  and Jeff White. 
Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Mr. Johnson and Dr. Rinne both declared a conflict of 
interest and vacated council chambers. Upon re quest by Dr. Aldrich , and pursuant to support 
received by M r. Cook and Mr. Esquibel, item # 23(d) was removed from the Consent Agenda.  
Upon request by Mr. White, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and M r. Cook, item 
#23(h) was removed from the Consent Agenda. Mr. Roybal moved to approve remaining items, 
seconded by Mr. White. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present 
in council chambers.

City and fixing the amount of general and special taxes as part of the revenue required to meet the 
said appropriation, all for t he Fiscal Year beginning July 1, 2023 and ending June 30, 2024 – 
referred to Committee of the Whole. (Sponsor – Mr. Johnson)

sewer rates, tap and system development fees, administrative fees, service charges and penalties, 
and septic and commercial waste dump rates, as recommended by the Board of Public Utilities – 
referred to Finance Committee. (Sponsor – Dr. Rinne)

of Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from MR Medium -Density Residential to MUB 
Mixed-Use Business Emphasis for land located at Lot 29, Block 1, Eastridge – referred to Public 
Services Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. Cook)

updating the entire Major Street Plan Official Map with newly platted and built roads and with the 
most recent Master Transportation Plan (Connect 2045) and other recently adopted Master 
Transportation Amendments – referred to Public Services Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. Cook)

[CA] Renewal of Contract #6837 between the City of Cheyenne and Delta Dental for group 
dental insurance benefits for City employees (Various City Funds).  Motion (per Consent Agenda)

[CA] Consideration of Bid #S-19-23 for an Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and T C

& Sons Construction, Inc. for the 2023 Miscellaneous Concrete Project (2019-2022 Optional 1%

[CA] Consideration to Purchase between the City of Cheyenne and High West Energy for

Optional 1% Sales Tax Fund) – referred to Finance Committee.

of Cheyenne, Street & Alley Division (2019- 2022 Optional 1% Sales Tax Fund) – referred to 
Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #S -20-23 for an Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and 
Reiman Corp. for the Greater Cheyenne Greenway, Lions Park Greenway Spot Repair (2017 & 
2021 Specific Purpose Option Tax Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Consideration to Purchase between the City of Cheyenne and Stryker for a Lifepak 15 
Monitor/Defibrillator for the Cheyenne Fire Rescue Department (Wyoming Department of Health, 
Community Emergency Medical Services Grant) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Consideration to Purchase between the City of Cheyenne and Power Equipment Company 
for the repair of a Paving Machine for the Street and Alley Department (2019- 2022 Optional 1% 
Sales Tax Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Bar and grill liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for FAFO 
Restaurant Group LLC d/b/a Dog Haus Restaurant, 3838 Atkins St., Cheyenne, WY.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
[CA] Designation of Official Voting Delegate and Alternate Delegate for the City of Cheyenne 
at the June 8, 2023 Wyoming Association of Municipalities Summer Convention Business 
Meeting to be held in Cody, Wyoming.  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (Councilman Dr. 
Rinne, Official Voting Delegate; Mayor Collins, Alternate Voting Delegate). 
 
Urban Renewal Authority 2022 Annual Report.  Dr. Rinne moved to acknowledge receipt 
of the report, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. 
Aldrich and Mr. Johnson.  
 
There were no comments made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business:  Dr. 
Aldrich and Mr. White. 
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16- 4-
405(a)(iii), seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open 
to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the gover ning body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following staff members were in attendance: City Attorney, City 
Clerk, City Engineer and Mark Ste wart, Davis & Cannon LLP. (Clerk’s Note: executive session 
convened at 6:39 p.m.) 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:34 p.m. 
 
 
         
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-05-22 | CLIP_ID: 908 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 908

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
May 22, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins , COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich  (via Zoom) , Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave  and Jeff White.  
Absent: Bryan Cook.  Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  
Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. 
 
Expression of Appreciation of the 2022- 2023 Mayor’s Youth Council and State of the 
Youth Address was made. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on May 8 , 202 3.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Ordinance appropriating moneys for the City of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming for the various purposes in conducting the municipal government of said 
City and fixing the amount of general and special taxes as part of the revenue required to meet the 
said appropriation, all for the Fiscal Year beginning July 1, 2023 and ending June 30, 2024.  
Reporting for Committee of the Whole, Mr. Johnson moved to approve on second reading, 
seconded by Mr. Segrave. Mr. Johnson moved to amend to decrease the Capital Improvement 
Expenses line item in the Mayor’s Department General Fund expenditure by $105,000.00 and 
increase the Capital Improvement Expenses line item in the Miscellaneous Department’s General 
Fund expenditure budget by the same amount  and to increase the Special Distribution from the 
State line item in the Intergovernmental General Fund revenue category by $23,000.00 and t he 
Capital Improvement Expenses line item in the Miscellaneous Department’s General Fund 
expenditure budget by the same amount, seconded by Mr . Roybal .  Motion to amend carried.  
V oting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. Mr. Johnson moved t o amend to 
increase the Special Distribution from the State line item in the Intergovernmental General Fund 
revenue category by $50,000.00 and increase the Nuisance Abatement line item in the Compliance 
Department’s General Fund expenditure budget in the s ame amount, seconded by Dr. Rinne. 
Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Mr. Johnson 
moved to amend to increase the Special Distribution from the State line item in the 
Intergovernmental General Fund revenue c ategory by $127,280.00 and increase Parks Projects 
line item in the Miscellaneous Department’s General Fund expenditure budget in the same amount, 
seconded by Mr . Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present.  Mr. Johnson moved to amend to decrease the Miscellaneous Department “Transfer to 
Reserves” line item by $60,000.00 and increase the City Council Department as follows:  
$20,000.00 in the Ward 1 Contingency line item, $20,000.00 in the Ward 2 Contingency line item, 
and $20,000.00 in the Ward 3 Contingency line item , seconded by Mr . Laybourn. Mr. Johnson 
moved to amend the amendment to increase the “Transfer to Reserves” line item by $124,445.00 
for a total of $184,445.00 and increase the City Council Department as f ollows: $41,485.00 in 
Ward 1 Contingency line item, $41,485.00 in the Ward 2 Contingency line item, and $41,485.00 
in the Ward 3 Contingency line item for a total in each Ward Contingency Fund of $61,485.00, 
seconded by Mr. Laybourn. Motion to amend the amendment failed due to a tie vote.  V oting “yes” 
– Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. White.  V oting “no” – Mr. Johnson, Dr. Rinne, 
Mr. Roybal and Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend carried.  V oting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present with the exception of Dr. Aldrich who voted “no”. Main motion as 
amended (amended four times) carried.  V oting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. 
The following is a summary of the proposed budget including aforementioned ame ndments: 
General Fund , $66,215,278; Weed and Pest, $ 710,000; Youth Alternatives Grants, $930,480; 
Recreation Programs, $2,099,698, Surface Water Management, $6,028,882; Community 
Development Block Grant, $548,667; Law Enforcement Grants, $211,976; Miscellaneous Federal 
Grants, $929,612; Transportation Planning, $1,078,409; Transit, $3,560,683; Juvenile Justice, 
$170,745; Special Friends, $ 58,325; Development Impact Fees, $ 1,028,200; One Percent Sales 
Tax 2019-22, $13,089,895; Specific Purpose Option Tax, $574,456; Youth Activities, $ 19,207; 
Golf Facilities, $ 374,300; Fleet Maintenance, $ 6,389,579; Solid Waste Management, 
$19,985,856; Civic Center, $ 2,613,314; Ice & Events Center, $ 1,057,783; Perpetual Care , 
$27,000; Total City, $ 127,502,066; Water Enterprise, $ 28,772,400; Sewer Enterprise, 
$15,372,000; Total Appropriation, $171,846,746. 
      
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Ordinance approving and establishing revised water and

and septic and commercial waste dump rates, as recommended by the Board of Public Utilities .

 Pipeline Easement Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Samson Exploration,

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-06-12 | CLIP_ID: 910 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 910

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
June 12, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Richard Johnson, 
Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent: Ken Esquibel and Scott 
Roybal. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Mr. Johnson declared a conflict of interest and vacated 
council chambers.  Mr. Segrave moved to approve , seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried. 
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present in council chambers. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on May 22, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a retail liquor license application filed with the 
City Clerk’s Office for Cheyenne Regional Airport Board d/b/a Cloud Nine Restaurant & Bar, 300 
East 8th Ave., Cheyenne, WY.  Public comment was made by: Tim Bradshaw, Director of Aviation, 
Cheyenne Regional Airport . Comments were made by the following members of the governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn, Mr. Segrave  and Mr. 
White. There being no further comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, 
Mayor Collins closed the public hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15- 1-402 exist for about 2.06 acres of land located 
northwest of the intersection of Thomas Road and Ridge Road, Laramie County, Wyoming.  There 
being no comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed 
the public hearing. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15- 1-402 exist for about 1.69 acres of land located at 
4512 Rawlins Street, Laramie County, Wyoming (located south of Rawlins Street east of 
Cleveland Ave.).  There being no comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, 
Mayor Collins closed the public hearing. 
 
 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for proposed Fiscal Year 2024 budgets for the City 
of Cheyenne and Board of Public Utilities.  There being no further comments or input, and purpose 
of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing.        
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Ordinance appropriating moneys for the City of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming for the various purposes in conducting the municipal government of said 
City and fixing the amount of general and special taxes as part of the revenue required to meet the 
said appr opriation, all for the Fiscal Year beginning July 1, 2023 and ending June 30, 2024.  
Reporting for Committee of the Whole, Mr. Johnson moved to approve on third and final reading, 
seconded by Dr. Aldrich. Mr. Johnson moved to amend to decrease the Miscellaneous Department 
“Transfer to Reserves” line item by $5,000 and increase the Professional Development line item 
in the City Council Department by the same amount , seconded by Dr. Rinne.  Motion to amend 
carried.  V oting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. Mr. Johnson moved to amend 
to decrease the Miscellaneous Department “Transfer to Reserves” line item by $75,618.00 and 
increase various payroll expenditure line items in the Community Recreation and Events 
Department, Botanic Gardens Division, by the same amount, and to decrease the Specific Purpose 
Option Tax Fund Botanic Gardens Operations and Maintenance account payroll line items by 
$75,618.00 and decrease the Specific Purpose Option Tax revenue by the same amount, seconded 
by Dr. Rinne.  Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” –  all members of the governing body 
present.  Main motion as amended  carried.  V oting “yes” – all members of the governing body  
present. The following is a summary of the proposed budget including aforementioned 
amendments: General Fund, $66,215,278; Weed and Pest, $ 710,000; Youth Alternatives Grants, 
$930,480; Recreation Programs, $2,099,698;  Surface Water Management, $6,028,882; 
Community Development Block Grant , $548,667; Law Enforcement Grants, $ 211,976; 
Miscellaneous Federal Grants, $929,612; Transportation Planning, $1,078,409; Transit, 
$3,560,683; Juvenile Justice, $ 170,745; Special Friends, $ 58,325; Development Impact Fees, 
$1,028,200; One Percent Sales Tax, $13,089,895; Specific Purpose Option Tax, $498,837; Youth 
Alternatives Projects, $19,207; Golf Facilities, $ 374,300; Fleet Maintenance, $ 6,389,579; Solid 
Waste Management, $ 19,985,856; Civic Center, $ 2,613,314; Ice &  Events Center, $1,057,783; 
Perpetual Care, $27,000; Total City, $ 127,626,727; Water Enterprise, $ 28,772,400; Sewer 
Enterprise, $15,372,000; Total Appropriation, $171,771,127.  Comment s were made by the 
following members of the governing body: Mayor Collins and Mr. Laybourn.  (#4504)  
      
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Ordinance approving and establishing revised water and 
sewer rates, tap and system development fees, administrative fees, service charges and penalties, 
and septic and commercial waste dump rates, as recommended by the Board of Public Utilities .  
Reporting for Finance Committee, Mr. White moved to approve on thir d and final  reading, 
seconded by Dr. Aldrich .  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  
present. Comments were made by the following member s of the governing body: Mr. Laybourn 
and Dr. Rinne. (#4505)   
 
 
              
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Ordinance amending the Official Zoning Map of the City 
of Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from MR Medium -Density Residential to MUB 
Mixed-Use Business Emphasis for land located at Lot 29, Block 1, Eastridge. Reporting for Public 
Services Committee, M r. Cook moved to approve on thir d and final reading, seconded by Mr.  
Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “ yes” – all members of the governing body present with the 
exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no”. (#4506)  
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending the Major Street Plan Official Map 2017, by 
updating the entire Major Street Plan Official Map with newly platted and built roads and with the 
most recent Master Transportation Plan (Connect 2045) and other recently adopted Master 
Transportation Amendments.  Dr. Rinne moved to approve on thir d and final reading, seconded 
by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  
Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: Mr. Segrave. (#4507)  
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Section 5.12.030, Licenses and Permits -
Types, of Chapter 5.12, Alcoholic Liquor and Malt Beverages, of Title 5, Business Licenses and 
Regulations, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming.  Mr. White moved to 
approve on second reading, seconded by Mr . Segrave. Mr. White moved to amend by substitute 
dated May 26, 2023, seconded by Mr . Segrave.  Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body present with the exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no”.  
Main motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present with the exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no”. 
              
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Creating Chapter 2.96, Et hical Obligations of Public 
Officials, Public Members and City Employees, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the 
Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, specifying the ethical duties and obligations 
of Public Officials, Public Members and Employees of the City of Cheyenne .  Mr. Cook advised 
there was no recommendation from Public Services Committee.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on 
second reading, seconded by M r. Segrave.  Dr. Aldrich moved to postpone to June 26, 2023, 
seconded by Mr. Laybo urn.   Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present with the exception of Dr. Rinne voting “no”.  Public comment was made 
by: Seth Lloyd, Cheyenne Public Employees Association.  Comments were made by the following 
members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn and Dr. Rinne.

ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of

 Request to extend the time period in which Westby Edge LLC has to become operational 
for an additional year for their bar and grill and microbrewery licenses, located at 714 W. 20th St., 
Cheyenne, WY.  Mr. White moved to approve the extension of the microbrewery license, seconded 
by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Johnson 
and Mr. White. 
 
[CA] Request to amend a previously approved outdoor seating area for consumption relating to  
the City of Cheyenne Bar & Grill Liquor License held by Paris West  Restaurant and Bar, located 
at 1719 Central Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
There were no comments made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held June 26, 2023 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for the City of Cheyenne’s Amended Program Year 2020 Consolidated Plan prepared by the 
Cheyenne Housing and Community Development Office.    
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held June 26, 2023 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a bar and grill liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Wyoming Downs 
OTB14, LLC d/b/a Wyoming Downs OTB14, LLC, 1345 Dell Range Blvd., Cheyenne, WY.     
 
Announcement was made that due to the Juneteenth holiday on Monday, June 19, 2023, 
regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will instead be he ld as follows: Finance 
Committee will meet at Noon on Tuesday, June 20, 2023 and Public Services Committee will meet 
at Noon on Wednesday, June 21, 2023.  Both meetings will be held at 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Room 
104 and via electronic conference meeting. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business:  Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Johnson and Mr. Laybourn. 
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16- 4-
405(a)(vii), seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body present.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open to 
the public and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following staff members were  in attendance: City Attorney, City 
Clerk, City Engineer, Chief of Cheyenne Fire Rescue, City Treasurer and Kipp Sanders, Cheyenne 
Fire Rescue. (Clerk’s Note: executive session convened at 7:16 p.m.) 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:44 p.m. 
        
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-06-26 | CLIP_ID: 912 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 912

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
June 26, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich (via Zoom), Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel, Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
  
 Mayor Collins congratulated Dr. Rinne for being presented the Person of the Year award 
by the Greater Cheyenne Chamber of Commerce. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Upon request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support 
received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Segrave, items #22, 29(c), 30(a) and 31(d) were removed from 
the Consent Agenda.  Upon request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Mr. 
Segrave and Mr. White, items #29(h) & 29(i) were removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon 
request by Mr. Segrave, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Cook, item #29(j) 
was removed from the Consent agenda. Mr.  Laybourn moved to approve  remaining items , 
seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on June 12, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for the City of Cheyenne’s Amended Program Year 
2020 Consolidated Plan prepared by the Cheyenne Housing and Community Development Office.  
There being no comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins 
closed the public hearing. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a bar and grill liquor license application filed 
with the City Clerk’s Office for Wyoming Downs OTB14, LLC d/b/a Wyoming Downs OTB14, 
LLC, 1345 Dell Range Blvd., Cheyenne, WY .  Public comment was made by:  Tracy Laycock, 
Hirst Applegate, legal counsel for Wyoming Downs OTB14, LLC. Comments were made by the 
following members of the governing body: Mr. Cook and Dr. Rinne . There being no further 
comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public 
hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee.

Reappointments of Merle Smith and Richard Vallee to the Public Transit Advisory Board; 
Stefanie Reese and Danica Mrozinsky to the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority; 
Kenneth Dugas to the Urban Renewal Authority; JJ Chen to the Cheyenne -Laramie County 
Economic Development Joint Powers Board; Kathy Emmons, Jeffrey Merton O’Holleran a nd 
Carla Thurin to the Laramie County Community Juvenile Services Joint Powers Board; and 
Mackenzie Howard to the Community Action of Laramie County Board of Directors.  Mayor 
Collins noted Stefanie Reese’s appointment is not due for reappointment and removed her from 
the reappointments.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did 
not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
Appointments of Timothy Johnson and Joel Hickerson to the Laramie County Community 
Juvenile Services Joint Powers Board; Michael Rucker to the Public Transit Advisory Board; and 
Janelle Rose, Judy Moss, and Landyn Medina to the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority 
Board.  Mr. Laybourn moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body with the exception Mr. Johnson voting “ no” and Mayor 
Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
There were no comments made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
There were no comments made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made that a calcutta benefitting Cheyenne area charities will be held 
on Saturday, July 29, 2023 at 5:30 p.m. in the Cowboy Bistro, located beside the Rodeo Committee 
Cabin at Frontier Park, Cheyenne, Wyoming, in conjunction with the 2023 Cheyenne Frontier 
Days Rodeo (10% of proceeds go to bona fide charities).    
 
Announcement was made that due to the Independence Day holiday on Tuesday, J uly 4, 
2023, regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will instead be held as follows: 
Finance Committee will meet at 10:00 a.m. Wednesday, July 5, 2023 and Public Services 
Committee will meet at Noon on Wednesday, July 5, 2023.  Both meetings w ill be held at 2101 
O’Neil Avenue, Room 104 and via electronic conference meeting. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business: Mr. 
Johnson and Mr. White. 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 9:02 p.m. 
 
        
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-07-10 | CLIP_ID: 914 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 914

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
July 10, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave  and Jeff White. 
Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Mr. Cook, and pursuant to support received 
by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Roybal, items #21 and 22 were removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon 
request by Mr. Laybourn and pursuant to support received by D r. Aldrich and Mr. Segrave, item 
#26(a) was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon request by Dr. Rinne and pursuant to support 
received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Esquibel , item #28(a) was removed from the Consent Agenda.   
Mr. Segrave moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried. Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on June  26, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
              
ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Creating Chapter 2.96, Ethical Obligati ons of Public 
Officials, Public Members and City Employees, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the 
Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, specifying the ethical duties and obligations 
of Public Officials, Public Members and Employees of the City of Cheyenne  (Postponed from 
June 26, 2023).  Reporting for Public Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved to approve on second 
reading, seconded by M r. Segrave.  Mr. Cook moved to amend section 2.96.050(A)(2) to strike 
the words “Advocate or” and in section 2.96.060 to strike in the 1st sentence “or the removal of a 
public official or public member from his or her office or position”, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  
Motion to amend carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception 
of Mr. Johnson and Mr. Roybal  voting “no”. Mr. Cook moved to strike section 2.96.040(B ), 
seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body with the exception of Mr. Johnson and Mr. Roybal voting “no”.  Mr. Cook, moved to amend 
existing City Code section 2.52.010 to insert “in Chapter 2.96 of city code or” after the phrase 
“Unless otherwise provided”, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend carried. Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Johnson and Mr. Roybal  voting 
“no”.  Main motion as amended failed due to a tie vote.  Voting “yes” - Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, 
Mr. Laybourn, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White.  Voting “no” Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Johnson, 
Dr. Rinne and Mr. Roybal.

Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. (#6334) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor and the City Clerk to sign a Final Plat for Sunrise 
Hills North Subdivision, a replat of a portion of Tract 25, Dell Range Addition, Laramie County, 
Wyoming (located northwest of and adjacent to the intersection of Thomas Road and Ridge Road).  
Mr. Cook moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body present. (#6335) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor and the City Clerk to sign a Final Plat for 
Christensen Park, 2 nd Filing, a Replat of Lot 15, Christensen Park, City of Cheyenne, Laramie 
County, Wyoming (located northwest of the intersection of Cribbon Avenue and Dodge Ct.).  Mr. 
Cook moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of 
the governing body present. (#6336) 
  
 RESOLUTION – To initiate annexation proceedings by the City of Cheyenne for an area 
generally located west of Interstate 25,  north of State Highway 210, east of Roundtop Road, and 
south of Horsecreek Road, commonly known as Francis E. Warren Air Force Base, Wyoming.  
Mr. Cook moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. White.  Mr. Cook moved to amend by substitute dated 
July 3, 2023, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body present.  Main motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body present. Public comment was made by: Michael White.  
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Mayor  Collins, Mr. 
Laybourn, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. (#6337) 
  
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the submission of an application by the City of Cheyenne 
for federal fund ing through the Transportation Alternatives Program administered by the 
Wyoming Department of Transportation for the purpose of construction for a Greenway path for 
pedestrians and bicycles from the Sun Valley Open Space into Kiwanis Community Park.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. (#6338) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Certifying Planning Commission action amending the Future Land Use 
maps of PlanCheyenne changing the land use category from Mixed-Use Residential to Mixed-Use 
Employment for property generally located west of Burlington Trail and south of and adjacent to 
HR Ranch Road – referred to Public Services Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. Cook)  
       
 Memorandum of Understanding between the Cheyenne Historic Preservation Board and 
the Cheyenne Masonic Building Association regarding the fulfillment of the match requirement 
for the Wyoming State Historic Preservation Office, Certified Local Government Gra nt awarded 
to the Cheyenne Historic Preservation Board for a building assessment on the Masonic Temple .  
Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by Mr.  Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present.  Public comment  was mad e by: Kelley Davis, 
representing Cheyenne Masonic Building Association.  Comments were made  by the following 
members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Roybal.  (#7789)  
 
[CA] Purchase and Sale Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Gysel Whitney, LLC for 
the future Storey Boulevard Right-of-Way (2023-2026 Optional 1% Sales Tax Fund).  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $555,780.00). (#7790)  
 
[CA] Utility Easement Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Board of Public 
Utilities for installation and maintenance of a water main on City owned property .  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. (#7791) 
 
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Summit Engineering 
for Engineering Design for the Sun Valley to Kiwanis Park Greenway Connector  (2021 Specific 
Purpose Option Tax Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Intergovernmental Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Laramie County School 
District #1 concerning joint use of facilities – referred to Finance Committee. 
  
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E -27-23 for the Transfer Station Lift Station Package and 
Equipment (Solid Waste Fund) – referred to Finance Committee.    
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E -28-23 for the Compost Facility Waste Hauling Project (Solid 
Waste Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
 Bar and grill liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Wyoming  
Downs OTB14, LLC d/b/a Wyoming Downs OTB14, LLC, 1345 Dell Range Blvd., Cheyenne, 
WY.  Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present. Public comment was made by: Traci Lacock, legal 
counsel for Wyoming Downs OTB14, LLC.  Comments were made by the following members of 
the governing body: Dr. Aldrich and Dr. Rinne.  
 
There were no comments made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made that the July 24, 2023 City Council meeting will be held in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, and via electronic conference 
meeting at Noon instead of 6:00 p.m. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business:  Dr. 
Aldrich and Mr. Johnson. 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:54 p.m. 
 
        
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk
