MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-07-24 | CLIP_ID: 916 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 916

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
July 24, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 12:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson (via Zoom), Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and 
Jeff White. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Dr. Rinne, and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Roybal and Mr. Segrave, item #20(b) was removed from the Consent Agenda. Mr. Segrave 
moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on Ju ly 10, 2023.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
              
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Creating Chapter 8.68, Camping in Public Places, of Title 
8, Health and Safety, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, Prohibiting the 
Act of Camping on Public Property as Specified.  Reporting for Public Services Committee, Mr. 
Cook moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Mr. Cook moved to 
amend by substitute dated July 17, 2023, seconded by Mr . Laybourn. Motion to amend by 
substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  with the exception of Mr. 
Johnson voting “no”.  Main motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no”.  Comments were made by 
the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins and Mr. Laybourn. 
(#4518)  
   
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from HI Heavy Industrial, AG Agricultural and 
County A -2 to LI Light Industrial for land located west of Burlington Trail and south of and 
adjacent to HR Ranch Road .  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by D r. 
Aldrich.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Defining the Terms and Conditions of Employment for 
sworn officers of the Cheyenne Police Department for the Fiscal Year 2023- 2024 – referred to 
Finance Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. White)      
              
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Creating Secti on 2.08.060, City Employees and 
Immediate Family Members; Conflict of Interest Policy, of Chapter 2.08, City Officers and 
Employees Generally, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the Municipal Code of the City 
of Cheyenne, Wyoming, requiring the promulgation of a Conflict of Interest Policy for employees 
of the City of Cheyenne as specified and making a conforming amendment to Section 2.52.010, 
Regulations and Benefits  – referred to Public Services Committee.  (Sponsors – Mr. Cook, Mr. 
Johnson, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Roybal)     
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Creating a new Section 15.50.070, Neglected Structure, 
of Chapter 15.50, Historic Design Guideline, of Title 15, Buildings and Construction, of the 
Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming – referred to Public Services Committee. 
(Sponsor – Mr. Johnson)   
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Certifying Planning Commission action amending the Future Land Use 
maps of PlanCheyenne changing the land use category from Mixed-Use Residential to Mixed-Use 
Employment for property generally located west of Burlington Trail and south of and adjacent to 
HR Ranch Road.  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried. (#6339) 
 
RESOLUTION – Expressing the support of the Governing Body for the Board of Public 
Utilities’ application to the Wyoming State Loan and Investment Bo ard for funding through the 
Local Government Project ARPA Grants Program for the Drinking Water Disinfection System 
Conversion Project in the amount of $4,463,000.00 and authorizing the Director of the Board of 
Public Utilities to execute the application and all related documents (Sponsors – Mr. Johnson, Dr. 
Rinne and Mr. White).  Reporting for Finance Committee, Mr. White advised the item was 
reviewed under other items for the agenda at the July 17, 2023 Finance Committee meeting, 
therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Code have been met.  Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded 
by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. (#6340) 
 
RESOLUTION – Authorizing the submission of a Local Government Project ARPA Grant 
Application to the State Loan and Investment Board on behalf of the Governing Body for the City 
of Cheyenne for the purpose of land acquisition of blighted property on 15 th Street for the 
development of Low-Income Housing (Sponsor – Mr. White).  Mr. White advised the item was 
reviewed under other items for the agenda at the July 17, 2023 Finance Committee meeting, 
therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Code have been met. Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded 
by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. Public 
comment was made by: Dan Dorsch, Director, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County. (#6341) 
  
RESOLUTION – Whereby the Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne authorizes a 
Special Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority District Election to be held October 18, 
2023, as provided by Wyoming Statute § 15- 9-217(b) which, upon affirmative majority vote of 
the qualified voters within the district, will authorize a Special Assessment of twenty (20) mills 
upon real property within the district, excluding real property used exclusively for residential 
purposes, for a period of four (4) years (Sponsor – Mr. Laybourn and  Mr. White).  Mr. White 
advised the item was reviewed under other items for the agenda at the July 17, 2023 Finance 
Committee meeting, therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Code have been met.  Mr. White moved 
to adopt, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Mr. White moved to amend by substitute dated July 20, 2023, 
seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion to amend by substitute carried. Voting “yes” – all members of 
the governing body.  Main motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: 
Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Laybourn. (#6342) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Expressing the support of the Governing Body to request the State of 
Wyoming to reconsider the 2022 House Bill 0148, Creating a Special Animal Control District  – 
referred to Finance Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. Johnson)    
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Expressing the support of the Governing Body to request the State of 
Wyoming to reconsider the decriminalization of cannabis and reform to the Wyoming Controlled 
Substance Act – referred to Public Services Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. Johnson)

State Legislature review, consider, and adopt  modifications to State Statutes to nullify covenants 
that restrict Waterwise Landscaping  or require installation of Water -Intense Landscaping – 
referred to Public Services Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. Johnson)     
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Directing City staff to research and provide a report regarding the 
property currently utilized as a detention pond at Highland Village Subdivision – referred to Public 
Services Committee.  (Sponsors – Mr. Laybourn and Mr. White)    
 
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Summit Engineering 
for Engineering Design for the Sun Valley to Kiwanis Park Greenway Connector  (2021 Specific 
Purpose Option Tax Fund) .  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed 
$108,085.00). (#7796) 
 
 Intergovernmental Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Laramie County School 
District #1 concerning joint use of facilities . Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by D r. 
Aldrich.  Mr. White moved to amend by substitute dated July 14, 2023, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  
Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  Main 
motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  
Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: Mayor Collins. (#7797) 
 
[CA] Contract between the City of Cheyenne, Laramie County Sheriff’s Office  and Laramie 
County for housing prisoners at the Laramie County Detention Center (General Fund) – referred 
to Finance Committee.    
 
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne Metropolitan 
Planning Organization (MPO) and Stantec Consulting Services, Inc for the Safe Routes to School 
Plan Update (MPO, TAP and Safe Routes to School Funds) – referred to Finance Committee.       
 
[CA] Contract between the City of Cheyenne, Office of Youth Alternatives and Laramie County 
for Youth Crisis Shelter Services (Revenue to the City) – referred to Finance Committee.         
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Rythm EFX, LLC, for 
Cheyenne Depot Plaza Christmas Light Design and Installation Project (2021 Specific Purpose 
Option Tax Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Development Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Tru-Grit Land Development, 
LLC, to outline the responsibilities for public infrastructure improvements for the Harmony Valley 
Subdivision (2023-2026 1% Sales Tax Funding and Private Development Funds) – referred to 
Finance Committee.       
 
[CA] Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Rotary Club 
Foundation regarding donation of Rotary Park to the City of Cheyenne – referred to Public 
Services Committee. 
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E -27-23 for the Transfer Station Lift Station Package and 
Equipment (Solid Waste Fund) .  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (accepted bid from 
Advanced Pump and Equipment d/b/a Russell Industries, Casper, WY, in an amount not to exceed 
$127,451.40).   
 
 Consideration of Bid #E -28-23 for the Compost Facility Waste Hauling Project (Solid 
Waste Fund).  Mr. White moved to accept  the bid from Pine Bluffs Gravel and Excavating, Pine 
Bluffs, WY, in an amount not to exceed $112,900.00, seconded by Mr. Esquibel. Motion carried. 
Voting “yes” – all members of t he governing body. Comments were made by the following 
members of the governing body: Mayor Collins and Dr. Rinne.    
 
 Appointments of JC Manalo to the Public Transit Board and Justin Huntley to the Housing 
and Community Development Advisory Council. Mr. Segrave moved to approve, seconded by Dr. 
Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present with the 
exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
There were no comments made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
There were no comments made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 12:19 p.m.

        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk

Notice of Emergency Procurement.  In accordance with the City of Cheyenne Purchasing 
Policy, an Emergency Procurement has been authorized by Mayor Collins for the Emergency 
Pioneer Park Sanitary Sewer Repair. The emergency repair has been authorized to Master 
Mechanical, for a total of $78,216.10. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held on August 28, 2023 at 6:00 p.m. 
in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference 
meeting, to receive comments and input for the West Downtown Urban Renewal Plan and Project 
as required by Wyoming Statute § 15-9-109. 
 
Public comment was made by the following individual under other business: Seth Lloyd. 
  
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business: Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel and Mr. Johnson. 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:20 p.m. 
   
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  August 23, 2023

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-08-28 | CLIP_ID: 919 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 919

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
August 28, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White .  
Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Mr. Segrave moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  
Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on August 14, 202 3.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
               
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to receive comments and input for the West 
Downtown Urban Renewal Plan and Project as required by Wyoming Statute § 15- 9-109.  There 
being no comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins  closed 
the public hearing. 
 
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Defining the Terms and Conditions of Employment for 
sworn officers of the Cheyenne Police Department for the Fiscal Year 2023 -2024.  Reporting for 
Finance Committee, Mr.  White moved to approve on thir d and final reading, seconded by Mr . 
Esquibel. Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. (#4520)  
              
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Creating Section 2.08.060, City Employees and 
Immediate Family Members; Conflict of Interest Policy, of Chapter 2.08, City Officers and 
Employees Generally, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the Municipal Code of the City 
of Cheyenne, Wyoming, requiring the promulgation of a Conflict of Interest Policy for employees 
of the City of Cheyenne as specified and making a conforming amendment to Section 2.52.010, 
Regulations and Benefits.  Reporting for Public Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved to approve 
on third and final reading, seconded by Dr. Rinne.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body. (#4521)  
 
 
 
 
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Creating a new Section 15.50.070, Neglected Structure, 
of Chapter 15.50, Historic Design Guideline, of Title 15, Buildings and Construction, of the 
Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on thir d and 
final reading, seconded by Mr. Johnson. Mr. Laybourn moved to postpone to September 11, 2023, 
seconded by Mr. White.  Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” all members of the governing 
body. Public comments were made by: Milward Simpson , Historic Preservation Board , Kathy 
Scigliano and Maeke Ermarth.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Roybal. 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Submitting to the qualified property owners of real 
property within the boundaries of the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority District of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming, excluding real property used exclusively for residential purposes, at a 
Special Election on Wednesday, October 18, 2023, the following question: ‘Shall a Special 
Assessment of twenty (2 0) mills be levied upon the assessed value of real  property within the 
boundaries of the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority District, excluding real property 
used exclusively for residential purposes, for a period of four (4) years pursuant to the provisions 
of Wyo. Stat. § 15 -9-217(b) for the purposes of (1) Landscaping or maintenance of public areas; 
(2) Planning or management of development or improvement activities; (3) Promotion of public 
events; and (4) Activities in support of business recruitment and development?’.  Mr. White moved 
to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body. Comment was made by the following member of the governing 
body: Mr. Laybourn. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from Frontier Mall PUD - Planned Unit Development 
(Ordinance No. 3132) to CB - Community Business for land located at Lot 7B, Block 1, Frontier 
Mall).  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. Public comment was made by: Shane Hansen, 
agent for the applicant, Steil Surveying Services . Comment was made by the following member 
of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Pursuant to Section 2.2.2 Planned Development -  
Regulating Plan or Development Master Plan and Section 5.6.2 PUD - Planned Unit Development 
District of the Unified Development Code, amending Ordinance No. 4286 by modifying certain 
standards of the adopted district, Planned Unit Development (PUD), for Sweetgrass (located 
southeast of the inte rsection of College Drive and Avenue C) .  Mr. Cook moved to approve on 
second reading, seconded by Mr. Roybal. Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Creating Chapter 8.68, Limiting the use of Plastic Single 
Use Carryout Bags, of Title 8, Health and Safety, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, 
Wyoming, regulating the use of single use plastic bags as specified.  Mr. White advised there was 
no recommendation from Finance Committee.  Mr. White moved to approve on second reading, 
seconded by Mr. Johnson. Mr. Johnson moved to amend by substitute dated August 25, 2023,  
seconded by Dr. Rinne.  Motion to amend by substitute carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body. Dr. Rinne moved to amend Section 3 by updating the effective date to July 1, 
2024, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion to amend carried with all members of the governing body 
voting “yes” with the exception of Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave voting “no”.  Dr. 
Rinne moved to amend Section 8.68.030 by deleting “environmental service fee” from the title 
and deleting subsections B-E, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion to amend failed due to a tie vote.  
Voting “yes” – Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Roybal and Mr. White.  Voting “no” – 
Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave. Main motion as amended 
by substitute, as amended failed. Voting “yes” – Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Dr. Rinne and Mr. 
Roybal.  Voting “no” – Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook, Mr. Laybourn, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White. Public 
comment was made by: Kathy Russell, Jacob Was serberger, Brian Pixley, Richard Lyle, Nancy 
Loomis, Mark Moody, John Young, Gail Young, Marilyn Burton, Dale  Steenburgen, Greater 
Cheyenne Chamber of Commerce, Evan Williams, Cathy Deister, Maeke Ermarth, Doug Powell, 
Kathy Scigliano, Darryl Orr, Adrian Crader, Kathy Selock, Steve Borin, Alan Shobin and Zachary 
Taylor.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, 
Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Esquibel, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White.  
 
Following a brief recess, Mayor Collins reconvened the meeting at 7:47 p.m. 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending Section 5.8.2.h of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), amending Manufactured Homes on Single Platted Properties or Tracts – referred to 
Public Services Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. Cook) 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending Section 15.08.031, Building Permit Fees -

Re-appointments of Wendy Soto and JC Manalo to the Housing and Community

There were no comments made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held on September 11, 2023 at 6:00

conference meeting, to receive public comments and input relating to the Federally Funded Dry 
Creek Drainage Master Plan Update. 
Announcement was made that due to City offices being closed for the Labor Day holiday 
on Monday, September 4, 2023, regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will 
instead be held in the Municipal Building, Room 104, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, 
and via electronic conference meetings as follows:   Finance Committee will meet Tuesday,

2023 at Noon.

Aldrich, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. White.

at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White .  
Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda.  Upon request by Mr. Cook, and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Esquibel and Mr. White, item #19 was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon request 
by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Mr.  White and Mr. Segrave, item #25(f) 
was removed from the Consent Agenda. Mr. Segrave moved to approve remaining items, seconded 
by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.

Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to receive public comments and input relating to 
the Federally Funded Dry Creek Drainage Master Plan Update.  Public comments were made by: 
Harry LaBonde , Jack Palma, Roberta Coates, Ed Heffern, Jeff Geyer, Laramie County 
Conservation District and Steve Achter.  There being no further comments or input, and purpose 
of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing.  
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Creating a new Section 15.50.070, Neglected Structure, 
of Chapter 15.50, Historic Design Guideline, of Title 15, Buildings and Construction, of the 
Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming .  Reporting for Public Services Co mmittee, 
Mr. Cook advised there was no recommendation from the committee.  Mr. Cook moved to approve 
on third and final reading, seconded by Mr . Johnson.  Dr. Rinne moved to amend paragraph B, 
section 12 to read: Review Committee means a group of three individuals that are nominated by 
the CHPB and approved by the City’s Governing Body.  Their purpose will be to investigate 
petitions filed with the Directo r alleging that a violation of this Chapter has occurred and to 
recommend to the CHPB if further action is necessary.  Ideally, the members of the Committee 
would have a background in Engineering, Real Estate, the Law or knowledge of Cheyenne’s 
history, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Main motion as amended failed due to a tie vote. Voting “yes” – Mr. Esquibel, 
Mr. Johnson, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Roybal and Mr. White.  Voting “no” – Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, 
Mr. Cook, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave.  Public comment was made by: Milward Simpson, 
Historic Preservation Board.    Comments were made by the following members of the governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Segrave and Mr. 
White. 
 
 ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Submitting to the qualified property owners of real

Cheyenne, Wyoming, excluding real property used exclusively for residential purposes, at a 
Special Election on Wednesday, October 18, 2023, the following question: ‘Shall a Sp ecial 
Assessment of twenty (20) mills be levied upon the assessed value of real  property within the 
boundaries of the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority District, excluding real property

events; and (4) Activities in support of business recruitment and development?’ .  Reporting for

ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Pursuant to Section 2.2.2 Planned Development -

Mary J. Lewark for the purchase of real property located at 700 Gettysburg Drive, City of 
Cheyenne, consisting of approximately 189,921.6 square feet, more or less and associated 
easement documents (2021 Specific Purpose Option Tax Fund).   Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried (in an amount not to exceed $245,000.00). (#7818) 
 
[CA] Contract between the City of  Cheyenne, Office of Youth Alternatives, and Laramie 
County to provide services for juveniles appearing in the Laramie County  Circuit Court and the 
Municipal Court of the City of Cheyenne for supervised probation, diversion, and the coordination 
of drug and alcohol services (Revenue to the City).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in the 
amount of $76,000.00). (#7819) 
 
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Volunteers of 
American Northern Rockies, Alcohol Receiving Center, to provide alcohol and drug detoxification 
services (General Fund).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in the amount of $136,875.00). 
(#7820)   
 
[CA] Human Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and United Way of Laramie 
County to provide services to various community programs (2023 -2026 Optional 1% Sales Tax 
Fund).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $750,000.00). (#7821) 
 
[CA] Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Safe  Harbor to 
provide a safe and secure environment to families who have been affected by abuse ( General 
Fund).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $20,000.00). (#7822) 
 
 
 
 Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Ayers Associates Inc 
for the West Crow Creek Greenway Plan (MPO Funds) .  Mr. White moved to approve in an 
amount not to exceed $134,997.54 , seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body.  Comment was made by the following member of the 
governing body: Mr. Laybourn. (#7823) 
 
[CA] Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Wyoming Housing 
Network, Inc. to provide financial assistance relating to the construction of affordable housing for 
the Robin’s Point Apartments Housing Project (Housing Loans Fund) .  Motion (per Consent 
Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $150,000.00). (#7824) 
 
[CA] Contract between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Housing Authority to provide 
supportive senior services (General Fund) – referred to Finance Committee.      
 
[CA] Lease agreement between the City of Cheyenne and GBP, LLC for the property located at 
6725 Faith Drive (Recreation Programs Fund) – referred to Finance Committee.       
 
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body related to the 
Voucher Report: Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Johnson.  
 
 Public comment was made by the following individuals under other business : Cade 
Batchelor and Ron Pinther. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business: Dr. 
Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White.  
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:07 p.m. 
 
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk

The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel,

Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to

item #20(b) was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. Segrave moved to approve remaining 
items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.

Appointments of Brian Bau to the Downtown Development Authority Boa rd; David

Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16- 4-
405(a)(iii), seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the

[Clerks note: Mr. Laybourn left the Zoom meeting at 6:43 p.m. following the vote of this item to 
join the executive session in person]         
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made that a Calcutta benefitting Make-A-Wish Wyoming, will be held 
on November 10 - November 11, 2023 at Red Lion Hotel and Conference Center, 204 Fox Farm 
Rd., Cheyenne, WY.  (10% of proceeds go to charity)     
  
 Public comment was made by the following indi vidual under other business : Doug 
Redding. 
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16- 4-
405(a)(iii), seconded by Mr . Esquibel .  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open 
to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the e xecutive session. The following were in attendance:   Stefanie Boster, City Attorney, 
Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk, Tom Cobb, City Engineer, and Mark Stewart, Davis & Cannon, LLP.  
(Clerk’s Note: executive session convened at 6:47 p.m.) 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:14 p.m. 
 
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kristina F. Jones, 
        City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  October 18, 2023

October 23, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Pete Laybourn, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent: Richard Johnson and Dr. Mark 
Rinne. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion. There will be no

in its normal sequence o n the agenda .  Upon request by Mr. Segrave, and pursuant to support 
received by Mr. Cook and Mr. Roybal , item #11 was removed from the Consent Agenda . Upon 
request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Esquibel, item 
#12 was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr . Roybal moved to approve  remaining items, 
seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” –  all members of the governing bod y 
present.

Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
 ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Amending certain Sections of Chapter 13.20, Wastewater

Cheyenne, Wyoming, regarding wastewater treatment and disposal.  Reporting for Public Services 
Committee, Mr. Segrave moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Esquibel. 
Mr. Segrave moved to amend by substitute dated October 13, 2023, seconded by Mr. Cook. Motion 
to amend by substitute carried. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present. Public comment was made by Brad Brooks, Director of Board of Public Utilities (BOPU). 
(#4528)

The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning

Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY

November 27, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel (via 
Zoom), Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion. There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Upon request by  Dr. Aldrich , and pursuant to support 
received by Mr. Johnson and Mr. White, item #18 was removed from the Consent Agenda. Upon 
request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Johnson, item 
#27(b) was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve remaining items, 
seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with 
the exception of Mr. Johnson and Dr. Rinne voting “no”.

(per Consent Agenda) carried.

on Public Right -of-Way; Permit Required, of Chapter 12.04, Streets, Sidewalks and Driveways 
Generally, of Title 12, Streets, Sidewalks and Public Places, of the Municipal Code of the City of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming and making a conforming amendment as specified . (Postponed from 
November 13, 2023).  Reporting for Finance Committee,  Mr. White moved to approve on third 
and final  reading, seconded by  Dr. Aldrich .  Mr. White moved to amend by substitute dated 
November 21, 2023 which includes the following motions made in committee:  amending Section 
“D” to strike “thirty (30)” days and replace it with “ninety (90)” days and amending section “D” 
and Section “H” to reflect the change and extend the total number of combined days to one hundred 
eighty (180) in any consecutive twelve (12) month period, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion to 
amend by substitute failed.  Voting “no” – all members of the governing body.  Mr. Roybal moved 
to postpone to December 11, 2023, seconded by Dr. Aldrich. Motion to postpone carried.  Voting

voting “no”.  Public comment was made by: Rick Fierro, Ram Trucking Inc. and James Bowers,

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-12-11 | CLIP_ID: 931 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 931

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 11, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White . 
Also present: Kylie Soden, Deputy City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion. There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Mr. Laybourn, and pursuant to support by 
Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Roybal, item #28(b) was removed from Consent Agenda. Dr. Aldrich moved 
to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. Segrave. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on Novem ber 27, 2023.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15-1-402 exist for about 5.91 acres of land located west 
of Polk Avenue and north of and adjacent to E Pershing Blvd. Public comment was made by:  
Benard Thyarks. Comment was made by the following member of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich 
and Mayor Collins. There being no further comments or input, and purpose of the hearing having 
been met, Mayor Collins closed the hearing. 
 
  ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Creating Section 12.04.240, Mobile Storage Containers 
on Public Right -of-Way; Permit Required, of Chapter 12.04, Streets, Sidewalks and Driveways 
Generally, of Title 12, Streets, Sidewalks and Public Places, of the Municipal Code of the City of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming and making a conforming amendment as specified. (Postponed from 
November 27, 2023).  Reporting for Finance Committee,  Mr. White moved to approve on third 
and final  reading, seconded by Dr. Aldrich . Mr. White moved to amend by substitute dated 
December 4, 2023, seconded by Dr. Aldrich. Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Johnson and Mr. Roybal voting 
“no”.  Mr. Segrave moved to amend subsection J to delete “For the purposes of this section, mobile 
storage container shall include roll off dumpsters” entirely and  insert “For the purpose s of this 
section, mobile storage shall not include roll off dumpsters” , seconded by Mr. Cook.  Motion to 
amend carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. Main motion as amended  by 
substitute, as amended, carried. Voting “yes” –  all members of the governing body. Public 
comment was made by:  Rick Fierro, Ram Trucking, Edward Ernste, Monument Homebuilders, 
Dan Dors ch, Executive Director, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County, Brenda B irkle, 
Executive Director, My Front Door, Joe Patterson, Homes by Guardian and Southeast Wyoming 
Homebuilders Association, Bruce Trembath, JEI Construction and Jason Stephen, Gateway 
Construction. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Cook, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Roybal and Mr. Segrave. (#4531) 
 
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from CB Community Business and HR High-Density 
Residential to MUB Mixed -Use Business for land located northwest of the intersection of Dell 
Range Boulevard and Whitney Road.  Reporting for Public Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved 
to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body. (#4532) 
                       
 ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, various 
tracts of land completely surrounded by current City limits situate in northwest Cheyenne generally 
located north of Dell Range Boulevard and west of Powderhouse Road.  Mr. Cook moved to 
approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Aldrich voting “no”.  Public comment 
was made by: Jim Weaver . Comments were made by the following members of t he governing 
body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave . 
(#4533) 
 
 ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne establishing the zoning classifications of AG Agricultural LR -  Low Density 
Residential, MR - Medium Density Residential, CB -  Community Business, and MUR -  Mixed-
Use Residential for land annexed to the City of Cheyenne generally located north of Dell Range 
Boulevard and west of Powderhouse Road.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final reading, 
seconded by Mr. Segrave. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  
(#4534) 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending subsection 6.7.5.b of the Unified 
Development Code (UDC), changing building façade design requirements to reduce required 
windows on urban loft multifamily residential and mixed -use residential development. Mr. Cook 
moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Johnson. Mr. Cook moved to amend section 
(b)(1) to delete “Where the interior operation or program of the building warrants screening from 
the street, a closed display window of at least 2.5 feet deep may be substituted along up to 50% of 
the linear distance of the façade”, seconded by Dr. Rinne. Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Segrave voting “no” .  Public 
comment was made by: Edward Ernste, Monument Homebuilders, Brenda Birkle, My Front Door, 
Ansley Mouw, Winters Griffith Architects, Dan Dorsch, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County, 
Jason Ste phen, Gateway Construction, Joe Patterson , Guardian Homes  and Tyson Pence . 
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor 
Collins, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. 
 
 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending subsection 5.1.5 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), removing minimum lot area requirements for detached dwellings, semi -attached 
dwellings, attached dwellings, and multi -dwelling buildings . Mr. Cook advised there was no 
recommendation from Public Services Committee.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second 
reading, seconded by Mr. White. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body with the exception of  Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave voting “no” .  Public comment was 
made by: Dan Dorsch, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County, Brenda Birkle, My Front Door, 
Heath Johnson, Jason Ste phen, Gateway Construction and Bruce Trembath, JEI Construction.   
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor 
Collins, Mr. Esquibel, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White. 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending subsection 5.1.5 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), removing density requirements for multi -dwelling buildings. Mr. Cook moved to 
approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. White. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave voting “no” .  Public 
comment was made by: Ansley Mouw, Winters Griffith Architects, Brenda Birkle, My Front Door,  
Jason Stephen, Gateway Construction and Dan Dorsch, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County.  
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending subsection 6.2.4 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), amending Table 6- 2, required automobile parking for multi -family development. 
Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. White . Mr. Cook moved to 
amend section (3) to strike “0.5 space per unit” and read “ 1.0 space per unit for studio or one 
bedroom”, seconded by Dr. Rinne.  Motion to amend carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body with the exception of Mr. Segrave voting “no”. Main motion as amended carried. 
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Segrave voting “no”. 
Public comment was made by: Jason Ste phen, Gateway Construction, Brenda Birkle, My Front 
Door, Dan Dors ch, Habitat for Humanity of Laramie County and Edward Ernste, Monument 
Homebuilders. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. 
Aldrich, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. 
 
 Following a brief recess, Mayor Collins reconvened the meeting at 7:51 p.m. 
                        
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Title 6, Animals, of the Municipal Code of 
the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, regarding the registration, control, impoundment and general 
care of animals and birds within the city .  Mr. Cook advised there was no recommendation from 
Public Services Committee. Mr. Cook moved to approve  on second reading, seconded by Mr. 
Johnson. Dr. Aldrich moved to postpone to December 21, 2023, seconded by Mr. Esquibel. Motion 
to postpone carried. Voting “yes”- all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr 
Aldrich and Mr. Laybourn voting “no” .  Public comment was made by: Britney  Tennant, CEO,

body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn, Dr. Rinne, Mr. Roybal 
and Mr. White.

No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
  
 Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held December 21, 2023 at 6:00 p.m. 
in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, and via electronic 
conference meeting regarding the required sale of real property and related equipment in Swan 
Ranch Industrial Park to Granite Peak Development Limited Partne rship upon a finding of 
beneficial economic development. 
  
Announcement was made that due to the Christmas holiday schedule, the regularly 
scheduled City Council meeting on Monday, December 25, 2023, will instead be held on 
Thursday, December 21, 2023 at 6:00 p.m. in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, 
Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting.  
 
Announcement was made that a Calcutta benefitting the Breast Cancer Initiative of 
Wyoming, will be held on January 5 – January 6, 2024 at Peppermill Bar, 1618 Stillwater Avenue, 
Cheyenne, WY. 
 
 
Mr. Segrave noted the motion to adopt the following Ordinance -  Amending Subsections 
6.6.3.b.3, 6.7.5.c, and 6.9.3.e of the Unified Development Code (UDC), changing the building 
façade design requirements for multifamily residential development, failed at the November 27, 
2023 City Council meeting.  Having voted on the prevailing side and pursuant to Chapter 2.04 of 
City Code, Mr. Segrave moved to reconsider the vote taken on the ordinance, seconded by Roybal.  
Motion to reconsider carried.  Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body.  Dr. Rinne moved 
to postpone to December 21, 2023, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to postpone carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body.  Comments were made by the following members of 
the governing body: Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business: Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White. 
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session Pursuant to Wyoming Statute s §§16-4-
405(a)(iii) and 16 -4- 405(a)(vii), seconded by Mr. Esquibel. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive 
session, not open to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as 
a result of the executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially 
conclude at the close of the executive session. The following staff members were in attendance: 
Stephanie Boster, City Attorney, Robin Lockman, City Treasurer, TJ Barttelbort, Purchasing 
Manager, Tom Cobb, City Engineer, Kylie Soden, Deputy City Clerk and Jason Sanchez, Director 
of Community Recreation and Events.  Also present: Attorney Mark Stewart.   
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 10:14 p.m. 
 
        Submitted by, 
 
              
        Kylie Soden, 
        Deputy City Clerk, CMC 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request.

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2023-12-21 | CLIP_ID: 933 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 933

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 21, 2023  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, (via Zoom), Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel, Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White. Also present: Kris Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion. There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Mr. Cook, and pursuant to support received 
by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Johnson, item #20 was removed from the Consent Agenda. Upon request 
by Mr. Johnson, and pursuant to support received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Cook, item #21 was 
removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve remaining items, seconded by 
Mr. Segrave. Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception 
of Mayor Collins who declared a conflict due to a business affiliation with agenda item #18. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on December 11 , 2023.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a public hearing regarding the required sale of real property and 
related equipment in Swan Ranch Industrial Park to Granite Peak Development Limited 
Partnership upon a finding of beneficial economic development. Public comment was made by Joe 
Stephenson, Granite Peak Development, Limited Partnership. Comment was made by the 
following member of the governing body:  Mayor Collins.  There being no further comments or 
input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the hearing. 
 
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending subsection 6.7.5.b of the Unified 
Development Code (UDC), changing building façade design requirements to reduce required 
windows on urban loft multifamily residential and mixed-use residential development. Reporting 
for Public Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded 
by Mr. Segrave.  Mr. Cook moved to postpone to January 8, 2024, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  
Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  Comments were 
made by the following members of the governing body: Mayor Collins and Mr. Johnson.   
 
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending subsection 5.1.5 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), removing minimum lot area requirements for detached dwellings, semi -attached 
dwellings, attached dwellings, and multi -dwelling buildings . Mr. Cook advised there was no 
recommendation from Public Services Committee.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final 
reading, seconded Mr. Johnson.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” –  all members of the governing 
body with the exception of Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave voting “no” .  Public comment was 
made by Jason Stephen, Gateway Construc tion, Brenda Birkle, My Front Door, Sam Weinstein, 
Southeast Wyoming B uilders Association and Eric Adams, Gateway Construction.  Comments 
were made by the following members of the governing body:   Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. 
Laybourn, Dr. Rinne and Mr. Segrave. (#4535) 
  
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending subsection 5.1.5 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), removing density requirements for multi-dwelling buildings. Mr. Cook advised there 
was no recommendation from Public Services Committee. Mr. Cook moved to approve on third 
and final reading, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body with the exception of Mr. Cook, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. Segrave voting “no”.   
Public comment was made by:  Jason Stephen, Gateway Constru ction, Brenda B irkle, My Front 
Door, Ansley Mouw, Winters Griffith Architects, Sam Weinstein,  Southeast Wyoming Builders 
Association, Wendy Volk, #1 Properties and Eric Adams, Gateway Construction.  Comments were 
made by the following members of the governing body:  Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Segrave. (#4536) 
  
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending subsection 6.2.4 of the Unified Development 
Code (UDC), amending Table 6- 2, required automobile parking for multi -family development. 
Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion carried. 
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Mr. Laybourn and Mr. 
Segrave voting “no”.  Public comment was made by:  Jason Stephen, Gateway Construc tion, 
Brenda B irkle, My Front Door , Ansley Mouw, Winters Griffith Architects, Sam Weinstein,  
Southeast Wyoming Builders Association, Wendy Volk, #1 Properties and Eric Adams, Gateway 
Construction.  (#4537) 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Title 6, Animals, of the Municipal Code of 
the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, regarding the registration, control, impoundment and general 
care of animals and birds within the city.  Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded 
by Mr. Roybal.  Mr. Cook moved to postpone to  January 8, 2024, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  
Motion to postpone carried. Voting “yes”- all members of the governing body.   Comments were 
made by the following members of the governing body:  Mr. Johnson and Mr. Laybourn.     
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Ordinance No. 4520 Defining the Terms and 
Conditions of Employment for Sworn Officers of the Cheyenne Police Department for Fiscal Year 
2023-2024. Reporting for Finance Committee , Mr. White moved to approve on second reading, 
seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body.   
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, land 
located east of Polk Avenue and north of and adjacent to East Pershing Boulevard. Mr. Cook 
moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” -
all members of the governing body. 
 
 
 
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne establishing the zoning classification of AR Agricultural Residential for land annexed 
to the City of Cheyenne located east of Polk Avenue and north of and adjacent to East Pershing 
Boulevard. Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion 
carried. Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body.   
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from AR Agricultural Residential to LI Light 
Industrial for land located east of Polk Avenue and north of and adjacent to East Pershing 
Boulevard. Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion 
carried. Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body.   
 
 ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Subsections 6.6.3.b.3, 6.7.5.c, and 6.9.3.e of 
the Unified Development Code (UDC), changing the building façade design requirements for 
multifamily residential development (Reconsidered from December 11, 2023).  Mr. Cook moved 
to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Mr. Cook moved to amend by substitute 
dated December 18, 2023, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend  by substitute  carried.  
Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Cook 
and Mr. Roybal voting “no”.  Main motion  as amended by substitute carried. Voting “yes” -  all 
members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Aldrich voting “no”.

 RESOLUTION – In accordance with Section 2D of the second amended and restated

(Sponsor – Mr. White) 
 
[CA] Professional Services Contract between the City of Cheyenne and Lance & Hall, LLP for

[CA] Purchase and Sale Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Taurus Holdings, LLC

Appointment of Anthony Laird and Darrell Hibbens to the Planning Commission; and 
Mark Moody to the Board of Adjustment.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  
Motion carried. Voting “yes” - all members of the governing body with the exception of Mayor

Reappointments of James W. Hearne to the City/County Joint Powers Board; Darin Westby

Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing  to be held January 8, 2024 at 6:00 p.m. in 
City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, and via electronic conference 
meeting to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute 
§ 15- 1-402 exist for about 9.39 acres of land at 5419 Ridge road (located northeast of the 
intersection of Douglas St. and Ridge Rd.) 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing  to be held January 8, 2024 at 6:00 p.m. in 
City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, and via electronic conference 
meeting to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute 
§ 15- 1-402 exist for about 29.61 acres of land east of Converse Avenue and south of Storey 
Boulevard.

held February 2 – February 3, 2024 at the Red Lion Hotel and Conference Center, 204 W. Fox 
Farm Road.      
 
Announcement was made that due to the New Years Day holiday on Monday, January 1, 
2024, regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will instead be held as follows:  
Finance Committee will meet at Noon on Tuesday, January 2, 2024 and Public Services 
Committee will meet at Noon on Wednesday, January 3, 2024.  Both meetings will be held at 2101 
O’Neil Avenue, Room 104 and via electronic conference meeting.        
 
The following member of the governing body made comment  under other business: Mr. 
Laybourn.   
 
Mr. Johnson moved to meet in executive session Pursuant to Wyoming Statute s §16-4- 
405(a)(vii), seconded by  Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open 
to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following staff members were in attendance:  Stefanie Boster, City 
Attorney; Charles Bloom, Planning & Development Director; Kris Jones, City Clerk; Seth Lloyd, 
Planning & Development; Brenda Moureaux, Deputy City Treasurer  and Jason Sanchez, 
Community Recreation and Events Director.      
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:35 p.m.
